- **narrate**: Offline voiceover for launch videos and explainers — a bundled Kokoro-82M TTS model turns a timed script into a narration track, reports each line's real duration so scenes can be fitted to the voice, and ducks the music under it. Also goes the other way: transcribe.py turns a recorded clip into word-by-word timings, so graphics can start on the exact spoken word. Use when /brag-slim runs with --voice or narration, whenever a video needs a spoken track without a cloud TTS API or key, and whenever graphics or captions must be synced to someone's own recording. ([Bhavya-Dhoot/brag-scaled-lite](https://github.com/Bhavya-Dhoot/brag-scaled-lite/tree/HEAD/skills/narrate))

- **narrative-design**: The craft of weaving story into interactive systems where the player is both audience and author.
